      file1 text: |RECORD=1|LibReference=ESDS__302/4|%UTF8%ComponentDescription=The ESDS302, ESDS304 devices are bidirectional TVS ESD protection diode array in two and four channel configurations respectively, for Ethernet and USB surge protection up to 12 A (8/20 Î¼s).
